# Skills · W · part 58

882 entries, workflow-engine-plugin-surface to workflow-kaltstart-und-routing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **workflow-engine-plugin-surface**: What the Claude Workflow Engine plugin ships, what it deliberately omits, and why a capability that looks missing is usually a recorded decision rather than a packaging defect. Use when a user asks why the plugin has no hooks, why an MCP-backed capability is unreachable after install, how plugin-internal paths resolve, or what a one-step install actually delivers. Keywords: claude-workflow-engine plugin, zero hooks, zero MCP servers, register-mcp, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT, plugin install surface. ([techdeveloper-org/ai-native-sdlc-engine](https://github.com/techdeveloper-org/ai-native-sdlc-engine/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/workflow-engine-plugin-surface) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **workflow-engine-setup**: Set up the Workflow Engine on a machine: a fresh Linux dev box (a cloud VM such as EC2) or a Mac. Opens with one short interview (at most two rounds of four questions) whose answers go into one answers file; its last question is what else the person needs, matched against the engine's feature catalog and shown as what it can do now, later, or not. Downloads the engine's installer with the user's own GitHub sign-in and runs its two passes: the machine pass installs the engine, then, once a board exists for one repository (shared lanes, the GitHub extension and a project plugin), the board pass… ([gnarlysoft-ai/skills](https://github.com/gnarlysoft-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engine-setup))
- **workflow-engineer-analyze-bug**: Diagnose one kind:bug issue read-only: reproduce it (browser MCP first, Playwright fallback, both against a booted stack), root-cause it to file:line, and post a `# Bug Analysis` comment proposing the fix + regression-test plan, then flip status → ready-to-review for a human to approve. Writes NO production code, creates NO branch, opens NO PR. Activate when dispatched with `Analyze bug #&lt;n&gt;` or '/workflow-engineer-analyze-bug'. ([MartinKChen/harness-claude-code](https://github.com/MartinKChen/harness-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engineer-analyze-bug))
- **workflow-engineer-diagnose-e2e**: Integrate origin/main, boot the slice's stack, run every E2E spec created or modified on the slice branch via testcontainers, and CATEGORIZE any failures into correlated production-fix groups — without writing a fix. Returns the diagnosis structurally (green | failures + groups | need-attention); the calling workflow dispatches one engineer per group. Edits no code or specs. Activate when dispatched with `Diagnose E2E acceptance for slice #&lt;n&gt;` or '/workflow-engineer-diagnose-e2e'. ([MartinKChen/harness-claude-code](https://github.com/MartinKChen/harness-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engineer-diagnose-e2e))
- **workflow-engineer-fix-bug**: Fix one approved kind:bug on its fix branch. Two dispatch verbs: `Fix bug #&lt;n&gt;` writes the regression test FIRST (RED per the approved # Bug Analysis comment, fails on pre-fix code), drives it GREEN, refactors, and pushes; `Fix the (gating|quality) review feedback on bug #&lt;n&gt;` addresses the newest # Bug Fix Gate Review / # Bug Fix Quality Review findings via TDD. Production code only; every commit carries `Refs #&lt;n&gt;`. Activate when dispatched with either verb or '/workflow-engineer-fix-bug'. ([MartinKChen/harness-claude-code](https://github.com/MartinKChen/harness-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engineer-fix-bug))
- **workflow-engineer-fix-e2e**: Fix ONE diagnosed E2E failure group on the slice branch via outside-in TDD on production code only — never the specs. The dispatch carries the group's root cause, failing tests, and fix hint. Write a failing unit/integration test mirroring the symptom, drive it green with the minimal change, propagate the class-of-bug to every sibling site, commit with a `Refs #&lt;slice#&gt;` trailer, and push. Activate when dispatched with `Fix E2E failures on slice #&lt;n&gt;` or '/workflow-engineer-fix-e2e'. ([MartinKChen/harness-claude-code](https://github.com/MartinKChen/harness-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engineer-fix-e2e))
- **workflow-engineer-fix-pr**: Fix merge-blocking issues on one open draft slice PR. Determine the scope (conflict, CI, or both) from live PR state, read every PR-issue comment newer than the slice branch's last commit, set up the slice worktree, merge main first to resolve conflicts, drive RED→GREEN for CI failures, commit with `Refs #&lt;pr-#&gt;` + `Refs #&lt;slice-#&gt;` trailers, push, remove `status:fix-in-progress`. Activate when dispatched with `Fix PR #&lt;pr-#&gt;` or '/workflow-engineer-fix-pr'. ([MartinKChen/harness-claude-code](https://github.com/MartinKChen/harness-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engineer-fix-pr))
- **workflow-engineer-fix-slice**: Address slice-review findings on one slice. Read the slice body and EVERY slice-review + human comment newer than the last `Refs #&lt;slice#&gt;` commit (union their findings — newest does not supersede older un-actioned reviews), set up the slice worktree, drive TDD per findings (production code only — never modify E2E specs), commit with `Refs #&lt;slice#&gt;` + `Task: &lt;id&gt;` trailers, push, post a summary comment. Activate when dispatched with `Fix the review feedback on slice #&lt;n&gt;` or '/workflow-engineer-fix-slice'. ([MartinKChen/harness-claude-code](https://github.com/MartinKChen/harness-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engineer-fix-slice))
- **workflow-engineer-implement-task**: Implement the named backend/frontend task ids on a slice branch via outside-in TDD. Read the slice body, locate the task block(s) in the `## Tasks` checklist, set up the slice worktree, drive RED→GREEN→REFACTOR per task until each delivery is satisfied, tick the implemented tasks' checkboxes, commit per task with `Refs #&lt;slice#&gt;` + `Task: &lt;id&gt;` trailers, push, post a summary comment. Activate when dispatched with `Implement slice #&lt;n&gt; tasks &lt;ids&gt;`, or on '/workflow-engineer-implement-task'. ([MartinKChen/harness-claude-code](https://github.com/MartinKChen/harness-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-engineer-implement-task))
